([theneoai/awesome-skills](https://github.com/theneoai/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/persona/research/chemical-analyst) · ★204 · 2 copies)
- **chemical-annotation-confidence-assessment**: Use when when you have received chemical annotations from GNPS spectral library matching workflow and need to assess their reliability before downstream analysis (e.g., chemical explorer visualization, sample filtering, or comparative metabolomics). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-annotation-confidence-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-annotation-integration**: Use when after training a tandem mass spectrometry embedding model (such as MSBERT) on a reference spectral library (e.g., GNPS), apply this skill to confirm that the high-dimensional embedding vectors project into interpretable chemical space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-annotation-integration) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-annotation-matrix-construction**: Use when the goal is a repository-scale, file-level chemical annotation table (e.g., compound presence/absence across thousands of public MS/MS files) built from ReDU/GNPS library search output rather than from raw spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-annotation-matrix-construction))
- **chemical-annotation-matrix-construction**: Use when you have selected a subset of public tandem MS files from ReDU/MassIVE that have been processed through GNPS spectral library matching, and you need to organize their chemical annotations into a matrix (rows = files, columns = annotated features) before performing PCA or other multivariate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-annotation-matrix-construction) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-annotation-matrix-pca-analysis**: Use when you have a file-by-chemical annotation table (GNPS spectral library matches per MassIVE file) and want to reproduce ReDU's repository-scale, unsupervised PCA ordination of public tandem MS files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-annotation-matrix-pca-analysis))
- **chemical-annotation-retrieval**: Use when you need programmatic access to PubChem's chemical annotations or summary information (e.g., LCSS, solubility, experimental properties) for a single record, and PUG-REST is unsuitable for such annotation data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-annotation-retrieval))
- **chemical-attribute-retrieval-from-databases**: Use when a merged metabolite table keyed on InChIKeys lacks attributes such as CID, SMILES, RefMet name, HMDB/KEGG/ChEBI IDs, or chemical ontology and must be enriched before interstudy comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-attribute-retrieval-from-databases))
- **chemical-attribute-retrieval-via-rest-api**: Use when a merged metabolite dataset already carries InChIKeys (output of MMS Step 1) and you need per-metabolite chemical properties and cross-database identifiers to enable interstudy comparability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-attribute-retrieval-via-rest-api))
- **chemical-attribute-serialisation**: Use when a ViMMS 2.0 workflow has just sampled a synthetic chemical mixture (e.g. via ChemicalMixtureCreator with HMDB-backed or uniform m/z formula samplers) and the sampled chemical attributes need to be persisted for simulation, replay or ground-truth comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-attribute-serialisation))
- **chemical-biologist**: Think and work like an expert Chemical Biologist. Use when a task calls for Chemical Biologist judgment. Reasons from chemical genetics, ABPP/TPP/CETSA chemoproteomics, and SGC/Portal probe criteria; deconvolves phenotypic hits with PAINS/aggregator triage, inactive analogs, and genetic epistasis while treating colloidal aggregation, probe promiscuity, and degrader DC50/Dmax tag artifacts as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/chemical-biologist/skills/chemical-biologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **chemical-biologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Chemical Biologist (wet-lab / chemoproteomics / probe discovery & target validation): Reasons from chemical genetics, ABPP/TPP/CETSA chemoproteomics, and SGC/Portal probe criteria; deconvolves phenotypic hits with PAINS/aggregator triage, inactive analogs, and genetic epistasis while treating colloidal aggregation, probe promiscuity, and degrader DC50/Dmax tag artifacts as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-biologist) · ★13)
- **chemical-biologist**: Reasons from chemical genetics, ABPP/TPP/CETSA chemoproteomics, and SGC/Portal probe criteria; deconvolves phenotypic hits with PAINS/aggregator triage, inactive analogs, and genetic epistasis while treating colloidal aggregation, probe promiscuity, and degrader DC50/Dmax tag artifacts as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-biologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **chemical-biologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for chemical biologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/chemical-biologist))
- **chemical-bonding**: Ionic, covalent, and metallic bonding, Lewis structures, VSEPR theory, molecular geometry, polarity, and intermolecular forces. Covers octet rule and exceptions, formal charge, resonance, sigma/pi bonds, orbital hybridization, electronegativity-driven bond classification, VSEPR electron-domain and molecular geometries, dipole moments, and the four intermolecular force types (London dispersion, dipole-dipole, hydrogen bonding, ion-dipole). Use when predicting molecular shapes, bond properties, or physical behavior from molecular structure. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/chemistry/chemical-bonding) · ★71)
- **chemical-candidate-dereplication-and-platt-reranking**: Use when you have generated k candidate SMILES from MSNovelist's RNN decoder for a tandem mass spectrum and need to deduplicate, validate, and rank them to pick the top de novo structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-candidate-dereplication-and-platt-reranking))
- **chemical-chemical-interaction-network-construction**: Use when a set of significant metabolites (e.g. fold change ≥1, adjusted p &lt; 0.05) needs to be interpreted structurally as an interaction network, to identify hub metabolites and compare topology against a published network figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-chemical-interaction-network-construction))
- **chemical-chromatogram-perturbation**: Use when a simulated chemical dataset (pickled Chemical objects from ChemicalMixtureCreator) must be perturbed with reproducible RT offsets, systematic RT drift across successive injections, and random RT/intensity noise prior to fragmentation-strategy simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-chromatogram-perturbation))
- **chemical-class-annotation-aggregation**: Use when you have a final merged MAW candidates table (file_id_mergedResults-with-one-Candidates.csv) with CANOPUS/ClassyFire class assignments per feature and need to summarize which chemical classes and subclasses dominate the annotated features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-annotation-aggregation))
- **chemical-class-assignment-classyfire**: Use when you have computed low-dimensional embeddings (e.g., t-SNE coordinates) or clusterings of mass spectra and need to validate that the learned representation space organizes molecules by chemical taxonomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-assignment-classyfire) · 3 copies)
- **chemical-class-based-link-filtering**: Use when a genome–metabolome pairing workflow yields long candidate lists of GCF–spectrum links (e.g. from Metcalf scoring) and class-ontology annotations (CANOPUS/MolNetEnhancer for spectra, MIBiG-derived classes for BGCs) are available to prune unlikely matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-based-link-filtering))
- **chemical-class-based-metabolite-grouping**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics feature table contains redundant or chemically related features (e.g. correlated isomers, shared chemical class) that should be collapsed into single nodes prior to DNEA network construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-based-metabolite-grouping))
- **chemical-class-dictionary-term-mapping**: Use when ClassyFire/ChemOnt category assignments must be translated into another chemical ontology (ChEBI classes, MeSH headings) for cross-database comparison or annotation exchange. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-dictionary-term-mapping))
- **chemical-class-enrichment-analysis**: Use when an IDSL.CSA-aligned compound matrix annotated with InChIKey/SMILES/molecular formula meta-variables must be clustered into chemical families and statistically tested for class-level abundance differences between two sample groups (e.g. Crohn's vs healthy controls). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-enrichment-analysis))
- **chemical-class-enrichment-statistics**: Use when a batch of MS/MS spectra (e.g. .mgf exports from HILIC- or CSH-Q Exactive HF runs) has been assigned putative compound names at MSI level 3 and you need chemical-ontology class distributions or enrichment statistics rather than single-compound IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-enrichment-statistics))
- **chemical-class-fraction-quantification**: Use when you have a list of metabolites identified by NMR (Chenomx) or similar profiling and need to report the chemical-class composition as fractions/percentages of the total detected metabolome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-fraction-quantification))
- **chemical-class-grouping-analysis**: Use when an aligned annotated molecular formula table (from IDSL.UFA) contains many related formulas and the goal is to collapse them into homologous formula-class clusters (e.g., perfluorinated, halogenated, or lipid chain families) rather than treating each peak independently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-grouping-analysis))
- **chemical-class-mapping**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network (GML or GraphML format) and wish to annotate network nodes with chemical class labels to support metabolite family interpretation. Use it specifically when you need to overlay chemical classification schemes (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-metadata-integration**: Use when you have generated a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based workflow) and possess chemical class annotations (from GNPS library matching, ClassyFire, or other structural classifiers) that you wish to propagate onto network nodes and edges to enable chemical family-level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-metadata-integration) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-node-mapping**: Use when you have a GNPS mass spectral molecular network (in graphml or cytoscape format) and want to enrich its nodes with chemical class information derived from GNPS public library spectral matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-node-mapping) · 3 copies)
- **chemical-class-novelty-detection**: Use when when you have CANOPUS chemical class predictions for your samples and need to identify which extracts contain chemical classes absent from the literature for their species or genus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-class-novelty-detection) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-class-novelty-scoring**: Decision support for prioritizing natural extracts whose MS/MS-derived chemical taxonomy (CANOPUS/NPC classes) contains classes absent from literature reports for the same species or genus, as one of four components in Inventa's Priority Score ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-novelty-scoring))
- **chemical-class-ontology-lookup-scoring**: Use when NPLinker has produced candidate BGC–MS/MS spectrum links (e.g., via standardised Metcalf scoring) and you need to filter false positives by matching genome-based and structure-based chemical class ontologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-ontology-lookup-scoring))
- **chemical-class-propagation-onto-networks**: Use when a GNPS molecular network plus an MS2LDA experiment exist and molecular families lack GNPS spectral library matches, so NAP/DEREPLICATOR annotations and ClassyFire classes can be propagated onto nodes/edges to reach MSI annotation level 3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-propagation-onto-networks))
- **chemical-class-set-comparison-across-taxonomic-levels**: Use when you have a canopus_npc_summary.tsv from a recomputed SIRIUS project space plus taxon-level reported-class tables and want to flag extracts whose predicted chemical classes are novel to the species or genus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-set-comparison-across-taxonomic-levels))
- **chemical-class-taxonomy-integration**: Use when a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based) needs chemically interpretable node annotation — e.g., nodes colored by CF_subclass in Cytoscape — after spectral library/in silico annotation steps are complete. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-taxonomy-integration))
- **chemical-class-term-curation**: Reference for constructing or auditing a chemical classification dictionary — when you must choose preferred labels and synonyms for chemical classes (e.g. resolving 'Imidazolines' vs 'Dihydroimidazoles') rather than classify individual structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-class-term-curation))
- **chemical-classification-aggregation**: Use when you have a collection of standardized molecular structures (SMILES or MOL format) that have been submitted to ClassyFire and you need to collect and organize their classification results into a single CSV or JSON table for downstream analysis, validation, or retention time modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-classification-aggregation))
- **chemical-classification-benchmark-reporting**: Use when an article reports compound-classification counts per database (e.g. '&gt;60,000,000 molecules in PubChem') and these per-database thresholds must be compiled into a verifiable table or CSV without converting them to exact figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-classification-benchmark-reporting))
- **chemical-classification-scheme-validation**: Use when you have structural annotations from in silico tools (SIRIUS, CANOPUS) and spectral library matches from GNPS, but need to (1) reconcile conflicting or incomplete ClassyFire ontology assignments, or (2) substitute NPClassifier taxonomy when ClassyFire ontology data is unavailable from GNPS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-classification-scheme-validation) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-compound-metadata-extraction**: Use when you need to enumerate the compound library of a PeakForest instance (e.g. a MetaboHUB demonstrator) and extract per-compound metadata into a flat table for inventory, curation, or downstream annotation reuse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-compound-metadata-extraction))
- **chemical-compound-record-field-validation**: Use when batch-importing large compound lists into PeakForest from the compound-import template file (XLSX, Online Resource 2) and each row must be checked for the mandatory common name, InChI and InChIKey fields before creating compound cards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-compound-record-field-validation))
- **chemical-compound-retrieval**: ToolUniverse workflow — Chemical Compound Retrieval ([lamm-mit/scienceclaw](https://github.com/lamm-mit/scienceclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-compound-retrieval) · ★251)
- **chemical-compound-retrieval**: Retrieves chemical compound information from PubChem and ChEMBL with disambiguation, cross-referencing, and quality assessment. Creates comprehensive compound profiles with identifiers, properties, bioactivity, and drug information. Use when users need chemical data, drug information, or mention PubChem CID, ChEMBL ID, SMILES, InChI, or compound names. ([rbr7/MedClawMini](https://github.com/rbr7/MedClawMini/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-compound-retrieval))
- **chemical-coverage-analysis**: Analyser correctement la couverture chimique d'un groupe de plantes — quelles espèces ont une chimie décrite et pourquoi. À utiliser AVANT toute affirmation du type « ces plantes sont inexplorées », « cette lignée est sous-étudiée » ou « cette région est un angle mort chimique ». Contient les six contrôles qui invalident la plupart de ces affirmations. ([Oshinsu/plant-chemistry-skills](https://github.com/Oshinsu/plant-chemistry-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-coverage-analysis))
- **chemical-coverage-optimization**: Use when your LC-MS feature table is incomplete or has low chemical coverage because traditional peak extraction algorithms (e.g., standard XCMS workflows) systematically miss features at m/z and retention time positions corresponding to known suspect compounds in your database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-coverage-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-cycle-research**: 化工周期与化工产品量化投研分析技能。采用自上而下双轨投研范式：【阶段一：大宗化工全板块横向截面扫描雷达】一键对齐 8 大子板块（纯碱/PTA/MEG/甲醇/PVC/钛白粉/聚氨酯/制冷剂）的吨价差、边际成本击穿深度、库存时钟、海关出口象限与业绩弹性，输出截面综合打分与轮动排序；【阶段二：细分品种垂直深度爆破】解剖单一化工品多工艺成本曲线、供需剪刀差、四阶段库存周期与周期股估值反向时钟。当用户询问化工截面轮动、化工大盘扫描、周期拐点排行、化工品垂直深度研究、细分品类供需与价差、买卖点与标的选择时使用此技能。 ([aidenreed937/stock](https://github.com/aidenreed937/stock/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/chemical-cycle-research) · ★2)
- **chemical-data**: Inspect CAS Common Chemistry contract status and query substance records only when a provider-issued API contract is configured. Use for chemical names, CAS Registry Numbers, structures, and basic compound information; not literature, reaction, or materials-property discovery. ([luffysolution-svg/omnischolar](https://github.com/luffysolution-svg/omnischolar/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-data) · ★8)
- **chemical-database-candidate-selection**: Use when running VInSMoC on run.npanalysis.org and you must pick which chemical database to search against, or associate an uploaded spectrum file with a project so it can serve as input to a database-search task. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-candidate-selection))
- **chemical-database-crosslink-counting**: Use when a database release claims compound structure coverage (e.g. '1188 structures with SMILES', '1347→1860 BGCs with structure data') and you need to reproduce those counts and per-database cross-link tallies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-crosslink-counting))
- **chemical-database-curation**: Use when curating or auditing a natural product database where each BGC record has an associated product structure (e.g. MIBiG/PubChem links), and you need to detect entries whose linked SMILES/structure mismatches the biosynthetically predicted product. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-curation))
- **chemical-database-curation-audit**: Use when reproducing or verifying aggregate counts of a curated chemical database assembled from multiple public sources, or when reconciling per-source entry totals against published unique-molecule statistics after deduplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-curation-audit))
- **chemical-database-field-completeness-checking**: Use when a curated chemical/adduct database is distributed as structure files (.mol/.cdxml) plus a spreadsheet, and you must verify identifier columns are populated and reproducible entry-by-entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-field-completeness-checking))
- **chemical-database-query-and-matching**: Use when when you have mass-to-charge (m/z) values from mass spectrometry imaging or other MS experiments and need to assign molecular formulae with high precision, especially in spatially-resolved metabolomics where traditional LC-MS annotation methods are insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-database-query-and-matching) · 2 copies)
- **chemical-database-querying-and-retrieval**: Use when you have BioTransformer-predicted metabolite structures (in SMILES or InChI format) and need to identify which known compounds in public databases match those structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-database-querying-and-retrieval))
- **chemical-database-search-configuration**: Use when raw mass spectra (MGF, mzXML, or mzML) must be searched against a candidate chemical database (e.g., NPAtlas, COCONUT, PubChem) and the VInSMoC task parameters need to be set to match the input spectra's ion mode, precursor m/z, and expected adducts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-search-configuration))
- **chemical-database-structure-retrieval**: Use when a predicted Morgan fingerprint bit string from an HSQC spectrum (DeepSAT output) is available and candidates must be retrieved, ranked, and annotated against known-molecule fingerprint databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-database-structure-retrieval))
- **chemical-dataset-record-counting**: Use when a deposited .sdf dataset must be audited to confirm its record counts match published per-category totals, especially when annotations need deduplication or category classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-dataset-record-counting))
- **chemical-decontamination-filtering**: Use when extracting component areas from a spreadOut()-processed GC-MS hit table and you must purge chemicals that lack strong cross-sample library matches, PubChem records, or canonical SMILES before downstream standardization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-decontamination-filtering))
- **chemical-descriptor-extraction**: Use when you have standardized molecular structures (SMILES or SDF format) and need to generate a uniform feature matrix for machine learning models (e.g., retention time prediction). - **chemical-engineer**: Think and work like an expert Chemical Engineer. Use when a task calls for Chemical Engineer judgment. Reasons from closing mass, energy, and momentum balances and competing equilibrium/kinetics/transport regimes through Aspen Plus/HYSYS flowsheeting, McCabe-Thiele and Damkohler-based sizing, pinch analysis, and HAZOP/LOPA with API 520/521 relief, while treating azeotrope-pinched columns, reactor runaway, recycle impurity accumulation, and pump cavitation (NPSHa&lt;NPSHr) as first-class failure modes. - **chemical-entity**: For working with any terms referencing chemical entities (CHEBI). CHEBI has some unusual properties that are counter-intuitive, so always use this skill to plan or check work that involves a chemical entity or CHEBI IDs, whether a new term, or significant alteration of existing terms ([geneontology/go-ontology](https://github.com/geneontology/go-ontology/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/chemical-entity) · ★256)
- **chemical-equilibrium-simulation-parameterisation**: Use when condensation temperatures (Tc) or molecule/element abundance-vs-temperature curves must be derived from a Gibbs free energy minimisation under a defined temperature grid, total pressure (e.g. 1e-4 bar), and stellar elemental abundance pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/chemical-equilibrium-simulation-parameterisation))
- **chemical-equipment-operator**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Chemical Equipment Operator/Tender — deciding whether to continue or stop a reagent addition when reactor temperature exceeds its expected range, evaluating a batch record deviation's actual chemical consequence before release, following lockout/tagout before servicing equipment that processed a hazardous chemical, or verifying secondary containment readiness. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/chemical-equipment-operator) · ★27)
- **chemical-equipment-selection-audit**: Audit and script chemical equipment design/selection calculations from DOCX/PDF/project reports, building a document-led equipment knowledge graph with formula families, parameter sources, manual decision gates, and evidence boundaries. Use when the user asks to read equipment selection reports, split modules, verify formulas, script calculations, classify Aspen/EDR/SW6/vendor/manual parameters, generalize calculation templates, or turn equipment-selection work into reusable knowledge/skills. ([milklong888/chemical-engineering-skills](https://github.com/milklong888/chemical-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/chemical-engineering-skills/skills/chemical-equipment-selection-audit) · ★11)
- **chemical-exposure-register**: Build an OEL/WEL/PEL-linked chemical hazard and exposure register, organised by similar-exposure group (SEG), banding each exposure with the risk matrix and flagging where health surveillance or air monitoring is due. Use it to build a chemical exposure register, link agents to occupational exposure limits, assess inhalation/dermal exposure risk by SEG, or plan a monitoring schedule. Every limit is cited with its source and year; controls are hierarchy-of-controls ranked; worker exposure data is de-identified to role/SEG labels before drafting. Decision-support only; a competent person (e.g.… ([ashley-eyekyam/hse-leadership-skills](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/hse-leadership-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-exposure-register) · ★3)
- **chemical-file-converter**: 化学文件格式转换工具。支持 .xyz, .gjf (Gaussian), .mol, .sdf, .pdb, .mol2 等格式互转。使用 Open Babel 或 RDKit 进行转换。当用户提供化学文件并要求转换格式、生成不同格式的文件、或提到文件转换时触发。 ([InternScience/ChemClaw](https://github.com/InternScience/ChemClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-file-converter) · ★53)
- **chemical-fluid-flow**: Use when analyzing fluid flow. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chemical-fluid-flow) · ★2)
- **chemical-formula-accuracy-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking formula-prediction methods on CASMI 2016/2017 or EMBL-MCF 2.0 spectra and you need comparable top-1/top-5 accuracy per tool and ion mode after excluding training-set overlaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-formula-accuracy-benchmark-evaluation))
- **chemical-formula-annotation-mapping**: Use when you have processed MSI peak data (in rMSIproc format) and need to distinguish matrix-related ions from analyte signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-formula-count-parsing))

- **chemical-formula-enumeration**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum with a measured precursor m/z and want to generate a list of plausible chemical formula–adduct pairs to rank or filter downstream. - **chemical-formula-manipulation**: Use when when you have a parent drug's raw chemical formula and need to predict its potential metabolites in mass spectrometry data (mzML format) to match against observed peaks. - **chemical-formula-prediction-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a de novo chemical formula predictor on MS/MS spectra (e.g., NPLIB1 test set) and you need reproducible top-1 accuracy values, especially as a function of how many MS2 peaks are visible to the model. - **chemical-formula-ranking-evaluation**: Use when after running formula inference on a benchmark dataset with known formula and adduct ground truth (e.g., NPLIB1, NIST20, or CASMI 2022). - **chemical-formula-stoichiometry-parsing**: Use when the input is a metabolite with a known chemical formula and the goal is a theoretical isotopic envelope (M, M+1, M+2, ...) under natural or SIP-enriched abundances. - **chemical-formula-subformula-classification**: Use when you have tandem mass spectra with known molecular structures (SMILES, InChI, or chemical formula) and aim to train or evaluate a formula-level spectrum predictor. - **chemical-formula-tokenization**: Use when you have collections of chemical formulae (e.g., from SIRIUS decomposition or subformula labeling) derived from MS/MS spectra and need to feed them into a transformer encoder. - **chemical-fragmentation-event-tracking**: Use when when running a ViMMS Environment simulation with save_eval flag enabled and you need to correlate fragmentation events in the output mzML file back to their originating chemical compounds for downstream evaluation, optimization, or validation of acquisition strategies. - **chemical-graph-parsing-and-featurization**: Use when a task requires converting molecular structures (SMILES/MolBlock/SDF from spectral libraries like NIST20) into molecular graphs and heavy-atom-only skeletons H(G) as input to bond-breaking fragmentation or GNN featurization. - **chemical-hazard-profile**: CAS number or chemical name → GHS hazard statements, signal word, pictograms and IARC cancer group from PubChem, plus EPA cancer calls and IRIS reference values from CompTox when a key is set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-shift-calibration-and-alignment))
- **chemical-shift-coordinate-extraction**: Use when you have loaded an INADEQUATE NMR spectrum file and need to detect individual peaks (local maxima) across the chemical shift dimension with associated intensity values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-shift-coordinate-extraction))
- **chemical-shift-data-formatting**: Use when you have collected or parsed 1H and 13C NMR peak data (chemical shift values and intensities) and need to submit it to the SMART 3 /api/smart3/search endpoint or similar TensorFlow Serving-backed molecular classification system that expects peaks as JSON rather than raw spectroscopic files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/chemical-shift-data-formatting))
- **chemical-shift-deviation-computation**: Use when you have paired experimental NMR mixture spectra and pure-component reference spectra and need to quantify how far identical peaks shift (ppm) between mixture and reference before applying shift-tolerant identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-shift-deviation-computation))
- **chemical-shift-reference-matching**: Use when when preparing an input spectral peak list for NMRformer or similar Transformer-based peak assignment models, and you have observed peaks whose chemical shifts do not align with known metabolite signatures. - **chemical-shift-roi-matching**: Use when user-defined 1H NMR ROIs (e.g., 18 ROIs over 0.5-4.5 ppm) must be screened against a reference metabolite library to list every metabolite with at least one chemical shift peak inside each ROI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-shift-roi-matching))
- **chemical-shift-table-extraction**: Use when a task requires per-atom assigned chemical shifts (residue, atom name, shift value) from a BMRB macromolecule or metabolomics entry, or when building CSV/NEF tables from NMR-STAR files. - **chemical-shift-variation-simulation**: Use when you need to verify that a spectral mixture-identification model is robust to chemical shift variation, by generating shifted spectral pairs and mapping the shift interval over which predictions stay above a threshold (here 0.5). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/chemical-shift-variation-simulation))
- **chemical-similarity-clustering**: Use when you have untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomic data (MS1 features with MS2 fragmentation spectra) preprocessed by MZmine2 into a feature table (BIOM format) and MGF file, and you want to construct a chemically-informed hierarchy of features for alpha/beta-diversity analysis or to group features. - **chemical-similarity-cutoff-filtering**: Use when running a Pickaxe/MINE-Database reaction-rule expansion that explodes in compound counts and you want to bias the network toward chemical space near defined target structures via a hard Tanimoto cutoff. - **chemical-similarity-network-edge-filtering**: Use when you need to connect metabolites lacking known biochemical intermediates via structural similarity: identifiers (names, KEGG, HMDB, InChIKey) must be converted to PubChem CIDs, fingerprints compared by Tanimoto coefficient, and edges filtered at a specified cutoff. - **chemical-similarity-network-generation**: Use when you have TOCSY/HSQC spectra for a sample set (e.g., prefractionated extract library) and need to cluster samples by shared structural features to dereplicate known scaffolds or locate bioactivity hotspots. - **chemical-similarity-scoring**: Use when sample-level NMR feature tables (spin systems with 1H/13C shifts) from multiple samples must be compared to build a similarity matrix for network generation or dereplication.
